> DEVELOPER

1. Payment status is good
Client said "After payment the status of pending and awaiting farmer is good" ✅ No change needed.

2. PhonePe is the main UPI app
Client said "I only have PhonePe, majority usage is PhonePe." Focus on making PhonePe work properly.

3. Consumer registration needed
Consumer always has to enter phone number every time to see orders. Client said "we have to create registration for consumers too" so phone number is saved automatically.

4. Location search not working
Client set location to Tadepalligudem, farmer location also set to Tadepalligudem, but 5km search shows zero results. Location distance calculation is broken.

5. Farmer can save produce without entering price
Validation missing — price field is not mandatory. Farmer saves listing with no price.

6. Disable COD for now
Client said "Disable COD for now" — remove Cash on Delivery option temporarily.

7. Payment screenshot should be mandatory
Client said "Better to make attachment mandatory if we proceed this way temporarily" — buyer must upload payment screenshot before submitting order.

8. Avoid WhatsApp step
Client asked "Is there a way to avoid sending order notification to farmer via WhatsApp?" wants it to happen automatically behind the scenes without opening WhatsApp.

9. Consumer and Farmer tabs both visible
When someone opens consumer link they see both Consumer and Farmer tabs. Client asked should we ask user their role first and show only relevant tab.

10. No way to switch back from Farmer to Consumer
When user switches to Farmer tab there is no way to go back or launch directly as farmer.

11. Order status wording needs change
When farmer confirms payment received it shows "Confirmed" and "UPI Paid" separately. Client said change "Approve" button to "Payment received and approved" — one combined status.

12. Retry payment option needed
When farmer says payment not received, consumer should get a "Retry payment" button to pay again.

13. Farmer must give reason when declining
When farmer declines order, reason should be mandatory (e.g. "stock finished", "not available"). Consumer should see that reason. Declined orders should NOT allow consumer to retry payment.

14. Farmer should be able to say payment received again after retry
After consumer retries payment, farmer should see updated status and be able to confirm again.. out of this how many we are implemented

3	import { useEffect, useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
4	import Link from 'next/link'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	
7	type PickupSlots = {
8	  days: string[]
9	  time_from: string
10	  time_to: string
11	}
12	
13	export type CartItem = {
14	  listingId: string
15	  qty: number
16	  name: string
17	  variety?: string
18	  emoji?: string
19	  pricePerKg?: number
20	  priceTier1Qty?: number
21	  priceTier1Price?: number
22	  priceTier2Qty?: number
23	  priceTier2Price?: number
24	  priceTier3Price?: number
25	  unit?: string
26	  stockQty?: number
27	  farmerId: string
28	  farmerName: string
29	  farmerPhone: string
30	  farmerVillage: string
31	  farmerSlug: string
32	  farmerPickupLocations?: string[]
33	  farmerPickupSlots?: PickupSlots | null
34	  farmerUpiId?: string
35	  farmerQrCodeUrl?: string
36	}
37	
38	export type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
39	
40	export type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
41	
42	const CART_KEY = 'yff_cart_v1'
43	const CONSUMER_KEY = 'REDACTED'
44	const CART_EVENT = 'yff:cart-change'
45	const UPI_PENDING_KEY = 'yff_upi_pending_v1'
46	const UPI_LAUNCHED_KEY = 'REDACTED'
47	
48	const readCart = (): CartState => {
49	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {}
50	  try {
51	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY)
52	    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CartState) : {}
53	  } catch {
54	    return {}
55	  }
56	}
57	
58	const writeCart = (next: CartState) => {
59	  localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
60	  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT))
61	}
62	
63	function getActiveTier(qty: number, item: CartItem): { price?: number; isDiscount: boolean } {
64	  const { priceTier1Qty, priceTier1Price, priceTier2Qty, priceTier2Price, priceTier3Price } = item
65	  if (priceTier1Qty == null || priceTier1Price == null)
66	    return { price: item.pricePerKg, isDiscount: false }
67	  if (qty <= priceTier1Qty)
68	    return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
69	  if (priceTier2Qty != null && priceTier2Price != null) {
70	    if (qty <= priceTier2Qty) return { price: priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
71	    return { price: priceTier3Price ?? priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
72	  }
73	  if (priceTier3Price != null) return { price: priceTier3Price, isDiscount: true }
74	  return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
75	}
76	
77	export function useCart() {
78	  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>({})
79	
80	  useEffect(() => {
81	    setCart(readCart())
82	    const sync = () => setCart(readCart())
83	    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
84	    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)
85	    return () => {
86	      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
87	      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)
88	    }
89	  }, [])
90	
91	  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {
92	    const next = { ...readCart() }
93	    const existing = next[item.listingId]
94	    const rawQty = Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty)
95	    const newQty = item.stockQty != null ? Math.min(rawQty, item.stockQty) : rawQty
96	    const merged = { ...item, qty: newQty }
97	    const { price } = getActiveTier(newQty, merged)
98	    next[item.listingId] = { ...merged, pricePerKg: price ?? item.pricePerKg }
99	    writeCart(next)
100	  }, [])

'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'

type ActiveTab = 'consumer' | 'farmer' | 'delivery' | 'moderator'

export default function GlobalNav({ activeTab = 'consumer' }: { activeTab?: ActiveTab }) {
  const { tx } = useLang()

  const tabs = [
    { key: 'consumer' as const, href: '/consumer', label: tx.consumerNav },
    { key: 'farmer' as const, href: '/farmer/dashboard', label: tx.farmerNav },
    { key: 'delivery' as const, href: '#', label: tx.deliveryNav, disabled: true },
    { key: 'moderator' as const, href: '#', label: tx.moderatorNav, disabled: true },
  ]

  return (
    <nav className="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-green-900 shadow-lg">
      {/* Logo row */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-green-800">
        <Link href="/consumer" className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="w-9 h-9 bg-green-700 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
            <span className="text-white font-black text-xs">YFF</span>
          </div>
          <div className="leading-tight">
            <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm block">YourFamilyFarmer</span>
            <span className="text-green-400 text-[11px]">యువర్ ఫ్యామిలీ ఫార్మర్</span>
          </div>
        </Link>
        <LanguageToggle />
      </div>

      {/* Role tabs */}
      <div className="flex">
        {tabs.map((tab) => {
          const isActive = tab.key === activeTab

          if (tab.disabled) {
            return (
              <span key={tab.key} className="flex-1 text-center py-2.5 text-xs text-green-700 font-medium">
                {tab.label}
              </span>
            )
          }

          return (
            <Link
              key={tab.key}
              href={tab.href}
              className={`flex-1 text-center py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-b-2 border-green-300'
                  : 'text-green-300 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </Link>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  )
}
```

1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import Link from 'next/link'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
7	import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
8	
9	type Order = {
10	  id: string
11	  produce_name: string | null
12	  quantity: number | null
13	  unit: string | null
14	  total_price: number | null
15	  pickup_location: string | null
16	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'
17	  payment_method: string | null
18	  payment_status: string | null
19	  decline_reason: string | null
20	  created_at: string
21	  farmer?: {
22	    name: string
23	    slug: string
24	    village: string
25	    upi_id: string | null
26	  } | null
27	}
28	
29	export default function ConsumerOrdersPage() {
30	  const { tx } = useLang()
31	  const [phone, setPhone]     = useState('')
32	  const [orders, setOrders]   = useState<Order[]>([])
33	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
34	  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false)
35	  const [error, setError]     = useState('')
36	  const [retryingId, setRetryingId] = useState<string | null>(null)
37	
38	  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)
39	  const canSearch = digits.length === 10
40	
41	  // Reset payment_status to 'pending' so the consumer can pay again. The order
42	  // remains in pending status; once the consumer claims payment, the farmer's
43	  // realtime subscription on the dashboard surfaces it for re-confirmation.
44	  const handleRetryPayment = async (order: Order) => {
45	    if (order.status === 'declined') return
46	    setRetryingId(order.id)
47	    const { error: err } = await supabase
48	      .from('orders')
49	      .update({ payment_status: 'pending' })
50	      .eq('id', order.id)
51	    setRetryingId(null)
52	    if (err) {
53	      setError(err.message)
54	      return
55	    }
56	    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => o.id === order.id ? { ...o, payment_status: 'pending' } : o))
57	    if (order.farmer?.upi_id && order.total_price) {
58	      const upiLink = `upi://pay?pa=${encodeURIComponent(order.farmer.upi_id)}&pn=${encodeURIComponent(order.farmer.name)}&am=${order.total_price}&cu=INR&tn=YourFamilyFarmer%20Order`
59	      window.location.href = upiLink
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  const handleSearch = async () => {
64	    if (!canSearch) return
65	    setLoading(true)
66	    setError('')
67	    setSearched(false)
68	
69	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
70	      .from('orders')
71	      .select('id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, payment_method, payment_status, decline_reason, created_at, farmer_id')
72	      .eq('buyer_phone', digits)
73	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
74	      .limit(50)
75	
76	    if (err) {
77	      setError(err.message)
78	      setLoading(false)
79	      return
80	    }

  const triggerPostPayment = useCallback(async (screen: UpiPaymentState, utrRef: string) => {
    setSubmittingResult(true)

    if (utrRef.trim()) updateData.utr_number = utrRef.trim()

    setSubmittingResult(false)
    setPaidDone(true)
  }, [clearFarmer])

  // Keep a ref to triggerPostPayment so visibilitychange handler is never stale
  const triggerPostPaymentRef = useRef(triggerPostPayment)
  useEffect(() => { triggerPostPaymentRef.current = triggerPostPayment }, [triggerPostPayment])

  // On mount: only restore payment screen when UPI app was actually launched.
  // If launched is missing the user just viewed (or closed) the payment screen — don't restore.
  useEffect(() => {
    const pending = localStorage.getItem(UPI_PENDING_KEY)
    const launched = localStorage.getItem(UPI_LAUNCHED_KEY)
    if (!pending || !launched) return
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(pending) as UpiPaymentState
      setUpiScreen(saved)
      if (!autoTriggeredRef.current) {
        autoTriggeredRef.current = true
        triggerPostPayment(saved, '')
      }
    } catch { /* ignore corrupt data */ }
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []) // intentionally runs once on mount only

  // Persist upiScreen to localStorage whenever it changes

export type Town = { name: string; lat: number; lng: number }

export const AP_TOWNS: Town[] = [
  { name: 'Tadepalligudem', lat: 16.8142, lng: 81.5288 },
  { name: 'Bhimavaram', lat: 16.5444, lng: 81.5216 },
  { name: 'Eluru', lat: 16.7107, lng: 81.0952 },
  { name: 'Narsapur', lat: 16.4347, lng: 81.6946 },
  { name: 'Tanuku', lat: 16.7551, lng: 81.6818 },
  { name: 'Kovvur', lat: 17.0128, lng: 81.7295 },
  { name: 'Palakol', lat: 16.5139, lng: 81.7342 },
  { name: 'Akividu', lat: 16.5826, lng: 81.3968 },
  { name: 'Chintalapudi', lat: 16.8319, lng: 80.9827 },
  { name: 'Polavaram', lat: 17.2487, lng: 81.6395 },
  { name: 'Jangareddygudem', lat: 17.1000, lng: 81.2833 },
  { name: 'Nidadavole', lat: 16.9000, lng: 81.6500 },
  { name: 'Undi', lat: 16.6500, lng: 81.6000 },
  { name: 'Denduluru', lat: 16.8833, lng: 81.4000 },
  { name: 'Pedavegi', lat: 16.9167, lng: 81.1167 },
  { name: 'Rajahmundry', lat: 17.0050, lng: 81.7799 },
  { name: 'Vijayawada', lat: 16.5062, lng: 80.6480 },
  { name: 'Guntur', lat: 16.3067, lng: 80.4365 },
  { name: 'Kakinada', lat: 16.9891, lng: 82.2475 },
  { name: 'Gudivada', lat: 16.4347, lng: 80.9947 },
  { name: 'Machilipatnam', lat: 16.1875, lng: 81.1389 },
  { name: 'Visakhapatnam', lat: 17.6868, lng: 83.2185 },
  { name: 'Tenali', lat: 16.2427, lng: 80.6440 },
  { name: 'Bapatla', lat: 15.9049, lng: 80.4674 },
  { name: 'Narasaraopet', lat: 16.2333, lng: 80.0500 },
]

export function haversineKm(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): number {
  const R = 6371
  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180
  const dLng = (lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI / 180
  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
    Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2
  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a))
}

export function nearestTown(lat: number, lng: number): string {
  let best = AP_TOWNS[0]
  let bestDist = haversineKm(lat, lng, best.lat, best.lng)
  for (const town of AP_TOWNS) {
    const d = haversineKm(lat, lng, town.lat, town.lng)
    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = town }
  }
  return best.name
}

export function formatDistance(km: number): string {
  if (km < 1) return `${Math.round(km * 1000)} m`
  if (km < 10) return `${km.toFixed(1)} km`
  return `${Math.round(km)} km`
}

// Look up a town in AP_TOWNS by name. Case-insensitive, also matches when the
// user-entered name contains a town name (e.g. "Tadepalligudem Rural" → matches
// "Tadepalligudem"). Returns null if no town matches.
export function townByName(name: string | null | undefined): Town | null {
  if (!name) return null
  const q = name.toLowerCase().trim()
  if (!q) return null
  // Exact match first, then substring match
  const exact = AP_TOWNS.find((t) => t.name.toLowerCase() === q)
  if (exact) return exact
  return AP_TOWNS.find(
    (t) => q.includes(t.name.toLowerCase()) || t.name.toLowerCase().includes(q),
  ) ?? null
}

// Resolve coordinates for a farmer in this priority:
//   1. farmer.lat / farmer.lng if both are valid finite numbers
//   2. nearest town match against farmer.location_name, then village
// Returns null if neither yields coordinates.
//
// Why this exists: many farmers save their profile without using GPS — they
// only type the village name. Without a fallback, the consumer's distance
// filter drops them entirely, so 5km searches return zero results even for
// farmers in the same town.
export function farmerCoords(farmer: {
  lat?: number | string | null
  lng?: number | string | null
  location_name?: string | null
  village?: string | null
}): { lat: number; lng: number; approximate: boolean } | null {
  // Defensive Number cast — Supabase numeric(10,7) can serialize as string
  const lat = farmer.lat == null ? NaN : Number(farmer.lat)
  const lng = farmer.lng == null ? NaN : Number(farmer.lng)
  if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lng) && (lat !== 0 || lng !== 0)) {
    return { lat, lng, approximate: false }
  }
  const town = townByName(farmer.location_name) ?? townByName(farmer.village)
  if (town) return { lat: town.lat, lng: town.lng, approximate: true }
  return null
}

    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'SMS service is not configured. Contact support.' },
      { status: 503 },
    )
  }

  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,

  // Invalidate any existing unused OTPs for this phone
  await supabase
    .from('farmer_otps')
    .update({ used: true })
    .eq('phone', digits)
    .eq('used', false)

  // Generate a 6-digit OTP, store with 10-minute expiry
  const otp = String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000))
  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()

  const { error: insertErr } = await supabase

-- ================================================================
-- YFF Feature Migration
-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run
-- ================================================================

-- 1. Harvest date on produce listings
ALTER TABLE produce_listings
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS harvest_date date;

-- 2. Cover photo for farmer profile banner
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cover_photo_url text;

-- 3. Farmer avatar/profile photo
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS photo_url text;

-- 4. Pesticide test certificate (photo URL)
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pesticide_cert_url text;

-- 5. Pickup schedule: {days: string[], time_from: string, time_to: string}
ALTER TABLE farmers
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pickup_slots jsonb;

-- 6. OTP table for secure farmer login
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farmer_otps (
  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  phone       text        NOT NULL,
  otp         text        NOT NULL,
  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
  used        boolean     DEFAULT false,
  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_phone   ON farmer_otps(phone);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_expires ON farmer_otps(expires_at);

-- RLS: only service-role key (used in API routes) can access
ALTER TABLE farmer_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 7. Payment method and status on orders (COD support)
ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_method text DEFAULT 'cod';
ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_status text DEFAULT 'pending';

-- 8. Farmer GPS location for distance-based discovery
ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lat numeric(10,7);
ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lng numeric(10,7);
ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS location_name text;

-- 9. Password hash for farmer login
ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS password_hash text;

-- 10. Farm gallery photos
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media (
  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  farmer_id   uuid        REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type        text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'photo',
  url         text        NOT NULL,
  caption     text,

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { scryptSync, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

function hashPassword(password: string): string {
  const salt = randomBytes(16).toString('hex')
  const hash = scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex')
  return `${salt}:${hash}`
}

function verifyPassword(password: string, stored: string): boolean {
  try {
    const [salt, hash] = stored.split(':')
    const hashBuffer = Buffer.from(hash, 'hex')
    const derived = scryptSync(password, salt, 64)
    return timingSafeEqual(hashBuffer, derived)
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}))
  const rawPhone: string = body.phone ?? ''
  const password: string = String(body.password ?? '').trim()
  const digits = rawPhone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)

  if (digits.length !== 10) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  if (password.length < 4) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password must be at least 4 characters.' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  // Look up farmer by phone (any stored format)
  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
    .from('farmers')
    .select('id, name, slug, phone, password_hash')
    .or(
      [
        `phone.eq.${digits}`,
        `phone.eq.0${digits}`,
        `phone.eq.+91${digits}`,
        `phone.eq.91${digits}`,
      ].join(','),
    )
    .limit(1)

  const farmer = farmers?.[0]

  if (farmer) {
    if (!farmer.password_hash) {
      // Existing farmer logging in for first time after migration — set their password
      const hash = hashPassword(password)
      await supabase.from('farmers').update({ password_hash: hash, active: true }).eq('id', farmer.id)
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, farmerId: farmer.id, farmerSlug: farmer.slug })
    }

    if (!verifyPassword(password, farmer.password_hash)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Incorrect password. Please try again.' }, { status: 401 })
    }

    await supabase.from('farmers').update({ active: true }).eq('id', farmer.id)
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, farmerId: farmer.id, farmerSlug: farmer.slug })
  }

  // New farmer — create account with this password
  const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)
  const slug = `f-${digits}-${rand}`
  const hash = hashPassword(password)

  const { data: created, error: insertErr } = await supabase
    .from('farmers')
    .insert({
      phone: digits,
      slug,
      name: '',
      village: '',
      district: '',
      method: 'natural',
      region_slug: 'tadepalligudem',
      active: true,
      password_hash: hash,
    })
    .select('id, name, slug')
    .single()

  if (insertErr || !created) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not create account. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, farmerId: created.id, farmerSlug: created.slug })
}
```

```
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  // Create admin client inside handler so missing env var doesn't crash the build
  const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null)
  if (!body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request' }, { status: 400 })

  const { listingId, farmerId, payload } = body

  if (!listingId || !farmerId || !payload) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing fields' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  // Verify this listing belongs to the farmer before updating
  const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from('produce_listings')
    .select('id')
    .eq('id', listingId)
    .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)
    .maybeSingle()

  if (!existing) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Listing not found or access denied' }, { status: 403 })
  }

  const { data: updated, error } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from('produce_listings')
    .update(payload)
    .eq('id', listingId)
    .select('id')

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
  if (!updated?.length) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Update failed — no rows changed' }, { status: 500 })

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })
}
```

Good — I now have the full picture. Here's the **secure e-commerce architecture** I'll apply across all steps:

## Security model (applies throughout)

**1. Auth**
- Reuse the project's existing pattern (Node `scrypt` + salt, `timingSafeEqual`) — no new deps
- Consumer passwords: `salt:hash` stored in `consumers_auth.password_hash`
- New `SESSION_SECRET` env → HMAC-SHA256 signed session token
- Token stored in **HTTP-only, Secure, SameSite=Lax cookie** (`yff_consumer`)
- localStorage holds only `consumer_name` + `consumer_phone` for UI display — **never trusted by server**

**2. Authorization**
- Every mutation/read is gated by a server API route that derives `consumer_id` from the **cookie**, not from the client body
- `orders` gets a `consumer_id` FK → fixes today's hole where anyone who knows your phone can read your orders
- Order proof access: signed Supabase storage URL (5-min expiry), only buyer or farmer

**3. Input hygiene**
- Phone normalization: strip non-digits, take last 10
- Password min 6 chars (display rule), bcrypt-equivalent strength via scrypt
- File uploads: type whitelist (`image/jpeg`, `image/png`, `image/webp`), 5MB cap, client compresses to 1200px @ 0.7 quality first
- Server rejects unknown payment methods; never accept `cod` if farmer's `cod_enabled = false`

**4. Anti-abuse**
- In-memory rate limiter on `/api/consumer/login` (5 attempts / 10 min / IP+phone)
- Unique constraint on `consumers_auth.phone` (DB enforced)
- All payment screenshots in a **private** Supabase bucket (no public read)

**5. DB hardening**
- New migration script: tables + indexes + RLS-enabled (service-role only via API routes, matching existing pattern)

---

## Revised step plan

**Phase A — quick fixes (no auth needed):**
1. Price mandatory (#5)
2. "Payment received and approved" combined wording (#11)
3. Farmer COD toggle (DB + dashboard settings + cart respects flag)

**Phase B — consumer auth foundation:**
4. Consumer auth: DB migration + `/api/consumer/register`, `/login`, `/logout`, `/me` + signed cookie session
5. Login/register modal triggered on "Add to cart" / "Place order"
6. Header greeting + logout in nav

**Phase C — orders tied to auth:**
7. `orders.consumer_id` FK + place-order endpoint uses session
8. Mandatory payment screenshot (private bucket + upload endpoint + DB column)
9. My Orders page (uses session, not phone input)
10. Order Details page (`/consumer/orders/[id]`) + Contact Farmer WhatsApp button

**Phase D — UX polish:**
11. "My Orders" button + pending count badge on home (only when logged in)
12. Role gate on first visit (#9)

---

**Starting with Step 1 — Price mandatory.** Tiny scope:

- `src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:1620` — extend the validation block: also require `price1` parsed as a positive number; error text `"Set a price for the first quantity tier / మొదటి ధర అవసరం"` 
- `src/lib/translations.ts` — add `priceRequired` key (en + te)
- The "Save changes" / "Publish" button stays disabled-on-error pattern that's already there

Shall I proceed with Step 1 now?

**Step 1 done.** ✅ Price now required (positive number) before publishing/saving a produce listing. Bilingual error: "Set a price for the first quantity tier / మొదటి తరగతికి ధర తప్పనిసరి". Type-check passes.

---

**Step 2 next — Combined "Payment received and approved" wording.**

Plan:
- File: `src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx`
- For UPI orders, the existing flow already auto-approves on "Received" — so the bug is purely visual (two badges, "Approve" button still appears when it shouldn't)
- Change "✓ Received" button label → `"✓ Payment received & approved / చెల్లింపు అందింది & ఆమోదించబడింది"`
- When `payment_status === 'completed'` AND `payment_method === 'upi'`, hide the separate "Approve" button entirely (the order is already approved)
- Replace the two badges (`"✓ UPI Paid"` + status pill) with a single combined pill: `"✓ Paid & Approved"` for UPI completed orders
- Add `tx.paymentReceivedApproved` (en + te)

Proceeding now.

  - Server-side: in any place-order endpoint we add later, reject `payment_method === 'cod'` if farmer's `cod_enabled === false`
- Add bilingual toggle labels to translations

875	  const handleFarmerGPS = () => {

877	      setLocError('Geolocation not supported on this device.')
878	      return
879	    }
880	    setLocating(true)
881	    setLocError('')
882	    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
883	      (pos) => {
884	        const { latitude, longitude } = pos.coords
885	        setFarmerLat(latitude)
886	        setFarmerLng(longitude)
887	        // Use village name as display name since we have it
888	        setFarmerLocationName(farmer.village || 'Farm')
889	        setLocating(false)
890	      },
891	      (err) => {
892	        setLocError(
893	          err.code === 1
894	            ? 'Location permission denied / లొకేషన్ అనుమతి లేదు. Please allow location in browser settings.'
895	            : 'Could not get location. Please try again. / మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'
896	        )
897	        setLocating(false)
898	      },
899	      { timeout: 15000, enableHighAccuracy: true },
900	    )
901	  }

903	  const handlePickFile = async (
904	    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
905	    setFile: (f: File | null) => void,
906	    setPreview: (s: string) => void,
907	    currentPreview: string,
908	  ) => {
909	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
910	    if (!file) return
911	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError(tx.pickImageFile); return }
912	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tx.imageTooLarge); return }
913	    setError('')

915	    const compressed = await compressImage(file)
916	    setFile(compressed)
917	    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(compressed))
918	  }

920	  const uploadProfileImage = async (file: File, pathSuffix: string): Promise<{ url: string | null; err: string | null }> => {
921	    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
922	    // Include timestamp in path so each upload gets a unique URL, busting browser cache
923	    const path = `${farmer.id}/${pathSuffix}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`

925	      .from('farm-images')

928	    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
929	    return { url: data.publicUrl, err: null }
930	  }

}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/password.ts",
  "content": "import { scryptSync, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'\n\n// scrypt with per-user salt — matches the existing farmer login pattern\n// Format on disk: \"salt_hex:hash_hex\"\nexport function hashPassword(password: string): string {\n  const salt = randomBytes(16).toString('hex')\n  const hash = scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex')\n  return `${salt}:${hash}`\n}\n\nexport function verifyPassword(password: string, stored: string): boolean {\n  try {\n    const [salt, hash] = stored.split(':')\n    if (!salt || !hash) return false\n    const hashBuffer = Buffer.from(hash, 'hex')\n    const derived = scryptSync(password, salt, 64)\n    if (hashBuffer.length !== derived.length) return false\n    return timingSafeEqual(hashBuffer, derived)\n  } catch {\n    return false\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "// Normalize an Indian phone number to its 10 trailing digits.\n// Strips +91 / 91 / 0 prefixes and any non-digit characters. Returns '' when invalid.\nexport function normalizePhone(raw: string | null | undefined): string {\n  if (!raw) return ''\n  const digits = String(raw).replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10)\n  return digits.length === 10 ? digits : ''\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/rate-limit.ts",
  "content": "// Tiny in-memory token-bucket-ish limiter. Per-process only — fine for a single\n// Vercel region; for multi-region scale we'd swap to Redis. Enough to slow\n// brute-force on /api/consumer/login until that's needed.\n\ntype Bucket = { count: number; windowStart: number }\nconst buckets = new Map<string, Bucket>()\n\nconst SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000 // 1h\nlet lastSweep = Date.now()\n\nfunction sweep(now: number) {\n  if (now - lastSweep < SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS) return\n  lastSweep = now\n  // Drop entries whose window has long expired (≥1h)\n  for (const [k, b] of buckets) {\n    if (now - b.windowStart > SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS) buckets.delete(k)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns true when the request is allowed (under the limit), false when blocked.\n * @param key identifier — combine ip + phone for login attempts\n * @param max max attempts per window\n * @param windowMs window length in ms\n */\nexport function rateLimit(key: string, max: number, windowMs: number): boolean {\n  const now = Date.now()\n  sweep(now)\n  const bucket = buckets.get(key)\n  if (!bucket || now - bucket.windowStart > windowMs) {\n    buckets.set(key, { count: 1, windowStart: now })\n    return true\n  }\n  if (bucket.count >= max) return false\n  bucket.count += 1\n  return true\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/session.ts",
  "content": "import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'\nimport type { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n\n// HMAC-signed session token (server-only). Stored in an HTTP-only cookie so\n// XSS cannot exfiltrate it. Format: <consumerId>.<issuedAtMs>.<hmacHexBase64url>\n//\n// We never put consumer name or phone in the token — those live in the DB.\n// localStorage holds the public profile copy used purely for UI display, and\n// the server re-derives identity from the cookie on every request.\n\nconst COOKIE_NAME = 'yff_consumer'\nconst TOKEN_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 30 days\n\nexport function getSessionSecret(): string {\n  const secret = process.env.SESSION_SECRET\n  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {\n    throw new Error(\n      'SESSION_SECRET env var is missing or too short. Set a 32+ char random string in your environment (e.g. `openssl rand -hex 32`).',\n    )\n  }\n  return secret\n}\n\nfunction b64url(buf: Buffer): string {\n  return buf.toString('base64').replace(/\\+/g, '-').replace(/\\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '')\n}\n\nfunction sign(payload: string): string {\n  const secret=[REDACTED]()\n  return b64url(createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payload).digest())\n}\n\nexport function createSessionToken(consumerId: string): string {\n  const issuedAt = Date.now()\n  const payload = `${consumerId}.${issuedAt}`\n  const sig = sign(payload)\n  return `${payload}.${sig}`\n}\n\nexport type SessionPayload = { consumerId: string; issuedAt: number }\n\nexport function verifySessionToken(token: string | undefined | null): SessionPayload | null {\n  if (!token) return null\n  const parts = token.split('.')\n  if (parts.length !== 3) return null\n  const [consumerId, issuedAtStr, sig] = parts\n  const issuedAt = Number(issuedAtStr)\n  if (!consumerId || !Number.isFinite(issuedAt)) return null\n  if (Date.now() - issuedAt > TOKEN_TTL_MS) return null\n\n  let expected: string\n  try {\n    expected = sign(`${consumerId}.${issuedAtStr}`)\n  } catch {\n    return null\n  }\n  const a = Buffer.from(sig)\n  const b = Buffer.from(expected)\n  if (a.length !== b.length) return null\n  if (!timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null\n  return { consumerId, issuedAt }\n}\n\nexport function getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req: NextRequest): SessionPayload | null {\n  const token = req.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value\n  return verifySessionToken(token)\n}\n\nexport function setSessionCookie(res: NextResponse, consumerId: string): void {\n  const token=[REDACTED](consumerId)\n  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, token, {\n    httpOnly: true,\n    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n    sameSite: 'lax',\n    path: '/',\n    maxAge: Math.floor(TOKEN_TTL_MS / 1000),\n  })\n}\n\nexport function clearSessionCookie(res: NextResponse): void {\n  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, '', {\n    httpOnly: true,\n    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n    sameSite: 'lax',\n    path: '/',\n    maxAge: 0,\n  })\n}\n\nexport const CONSUMER_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = COOKIE_NAME\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/consumer/register/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { hashPassword } from '@/lib/password'\nimport { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'\nimport { setSessionCookie } from '@/lib/session'\nimport { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  // Lightweight anti-abuse: 10 registrations per IP per hour\n  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'\n  if (!rateLimit(`reg:${ip}`, 10, 60 * 60 * 1000)) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'Too many sign-up attempts. Try again in an hour.' },\n      { status: 429 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)\n  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  const name = String(body.name ?? '').trim().slice(0, 80)\n  const phone = normalizePhone(body.phone)\n  const password = String(body.password ?? '')\n\n  if (!name) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please enter your name.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n  if (!phone) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n  if (password.length < 6) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password must be at least 6 characters.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n  if (password.length > 128) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password is too long.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Reject duplicate phone (UNIQUE constraint also enforces, but check first\n  // to give a clean error message)\n  const { data: existing } = await supabase\n    .from('consumers_auth')\n    .select('id')\n    .eq('phone', phone)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (existing) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'An account already exists for this phone. Please log in instead.' },\n      { status: 409 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  const password_hash = hashPassword(password)\n  const { data: created, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('consumers_auth')\n    .insert({ name, phone, password_hash })\n    .select('id, name, phone')\n    .single()\n\n  if (insertErr || !created) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'Could not create account. Please try again.' },\n      { status: 500 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  const res = NextResponse.json({\n    ok: true,\n    consumer: { id: created.id, name: created.name, phone: created.phone },\n  })\n  setSessionCookie(res, created.id)\n  return res\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/consumer/login/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { verifyPassword } from '@/lib/password'\nimport { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'\nimport { setSessionCookie } from '@/lib/session'\nimport { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)\n  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  const phone = normalizePhone(body.phone)\n  const password = String(body.password ?? '')\n\n  if (!phone) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n  if (!password) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter your password.' }, { status: 400 })\n  }\n\n  // Brute-force throttle: 5 login attempts per phone per 10 min, plus 30 / IP\n  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'\n  if (\n    !rateLimit(`login:phone:${phone}`, 5, 10 * 60 * 1000) ||\n    !rateLimit(`login:ip:${ip}`, 30, 10 * 60 * 1000)\n  ) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: 'Too many login attempts. Please try again in a few minutes.' },\n      { status: 429 },\n    )\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: user } = await supabase\n    .from('consumers_auth')\n    .select('id, name, phone, password_hash')\n    .eq('phone', phone)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  // Generic message either way — don't reveal whether the phone is registered\n  const generic = NextResponse.json(\n    { error: 'Incorrect phone or password.' },\n    { status: 401 },\n  )\n\n  if (!user) return generic\n  if (!user.password_hash) return generic\n  if (!verifyPassword(password, user.password_hash)) return generic\n\n  await supabase\n    .from('consumers_auth')\n    .update({ last_login_at: new Date().toISOString() })\n    .eq('id', user.id)\n\n  const res = NextResponse.json({\n    ok: true,\n    consumer: { id: user.id, name: user.name, phone: user.phone },\n  })\n  setSessionCookie(res, user.id)\n  return res\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/consumer/me/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest, clearSessionCookie } from '@/lib/session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)\n  if (!session) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ consumer: null }, { status: 200 })\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: user } = await supabase\n    .from('consumers_auth')\n    .select('id, name, phone')\n    .eq('id', session.consumerId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!user) {\n    // Cookie references a deleted account — clear it so the client logs out\n    const res = NextResponse.json({ consumer: null }, { status: 200 })\n    clearSessionCookie(res)\n    return res\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ consumer: user })\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/scripts/consumer-auth-migration.sql",
  "content": "-- ================================================================\n-- YFF — Consumer authentication\n-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run\n-- ================================================================\n\n-- 1. Consumer accounts table\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS consumers_auth (\n  id             uuid         PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n  name           text,\n  phone          varchar(15)  UNIQUE NOT NULL,\n  password_hash  text         NOT NULL,\n  created_at     timestamptz  DEFAULT now(),\n  last_login_at  timestamptz\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_consumers_auth_phone ON consumers_auth(phone);\n\n-- 2. RLS — only the service-role key (used in /api/consumer/* handlers) can\n-- read or write. The anon key used by the browser cannot touch this table.\nALTER TABLE consumers_auth ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\n-- No policies = no anon access. Service role bypasses RLS automatically.\n\n-- 3. Sanity check helper for ops debugging\nCOMMENT ON TABLE consumers_auth IS\n  'Consumer accounts. Server-only access via service role; passwords scrypt-hashed.';\n"
}

**Step 4 done.** ✅

Files created:
- `src/lib/password.ts` — scrypt hash/verify (constant-time compare)
- `src/lib/phone.ts` — phone normalization
- `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` — in-memory limiter
- `src/lib/session.ts` — HMAC-SHA256 signed cookie session, 30-day TTL, HTTP-only + Secure (in prod) + SameSite=Lax
- `src/app/api/consumer/register/route.ts` — name + phone + password ≥6, dedup check, sets cookie. 10 reg/IP/hour cap.
- `src/app/api/consumer/login/route.ts` — phone + password, generic error message (no user enumeration), 5 attempts/phone/10min + 30/IP/10min, sets cookie, updates `last_login_at`
- `src/app/api/consumer/logout/route.ts` — clears cookie
- `src/app/api/consumer/me/route.ts` — returns profile from cookie session; clears cookie if account was deleted
- `scripts/consumer-auth-migration.sql` — table + UNIQUE phone constraint + RLS enabled (service-role only)

**One required env var to add (in `.env.local` and Vercel):**
```
SESSION_SECRET=<run: openssl rand -hex 32>
```
The session lib throws a clear error if it's missing or under 32 chars.

---

**Step 5 next — Auth modal triggered on Add to Cart / Place Order.**

Plan:
- New component `src/components/consumer/AuthModal.tsx` — bottom-sheet style modal with two tabs (Login / Register), bilingual labels, form validation, calls `/api/consumer/login` or `/register`
- New hook `src/lib/useConsumerAuth.ts` — global auth state (calls `/api/consumer/me` on mount, exposes `consumer`, `login`, `register`, `logout`)
- On register/login success: stores `{id, name, phone}` in `localStorage.yff_consumer_v1` (display only) and updates global state
- **Gating:** intercept `addItem` in `useCart` and the "Place order" buttons in `Cart.tsx` — if no consumer in state, open the modal instead. After successful auth, retry the original action.
- Keep browsing fully anonymous (no modal until they try a mutation)

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import "./globals.css";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/lib/LanguageContext";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "YourFamilyFarmer — Natural food from farmers near you",
  description: "Buy natural produce directly from farmers in Andhra Pradesh. No middlemen.",
};

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  width: 'device-width',
  initialScale: 1,
  maximumScale: 1,
  userScalable: false,
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" className="h-full">
      <body className="min-h-full bg-gray-50 antialiased">
        <LanguageProvider>{children}</LanguageProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

'use client'

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { t, Language, TranslationKey } from './translations'

type LanguageContextType = {
  lang: Language
  setLang: (lang: Language) => void
  tx: typeof t.en
  bi: (key: TranslationKey) => string
}

const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType>({
  lang: 'en',
  setLang: () => {},
  tx: t.en,
  bi: (key) => t.en[key] ?? '',
})

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Language>('en')

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('yff_lang') : null
    if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'te') setLangState(saved)
  }, [])

  const setLang = (next: Language) => {
    setLangState(next)
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') localStorage.setItem('yff_lang', next)
  }

  const tx = t[lang]
  const bi = (key: TranslationKey) => (t[lang] as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? t.en[key] ?? ''

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, tx, bi }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  )
```

  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState, ReactNode } from 'react'\nimport dynamic from 'next/dynamic'\n\nexport type Consumer = { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string }\n\ntype AuthState =\n  | { status: 'loading'; consumer: null }\n  | { status: 'authenticated'; consumer: Consumer }\n  | { status: 'anonymous'; consumer: null }\n\ntype LoginPayload = { phone: string; password: string }\ntype RegisterPayload = { name: string; phone: string; password: string }\ntype AuthResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }\n\ntype ConsumerAuthContextValue = {\n  state: AuthState\n  consumer: Consumer | null\n  /**\n   * Run `action` immediately when authenticated; otherwise open the auth modal\n   * and run `action` after a successful login or registration.\n   */\n  requireAuth: (action: () => void) => void\n  login: (payload: LoginPayload) => Promise<AuthResult>\n  register: (payload: RegisterPayload) => Promise<AuthResult>\n  logout: () => Promise<void>\n  openAuth: () => void\n  closeAuth: () => void\n}\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'REDACTED'\nconst AUTH_EVENT = 'yff:consumer-auth-change'\n\nconst ConsumerAuthContext = createContext<ConsumerAuthContextValue | null>(null)\n\n// Modal is loaded only when first opened, keeping the initial consumer page light\nconst AuthModal = dynamic(() => import('@/components/consumer/AuthModal'), { ssr: false })\n\nfunction readCachedConsumer(): Consumer | null {\n  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null\n  try {\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)\n    if (!raw) return null\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw)\n    if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && parsed.id && parsed.phone) return parsed as Consumer\n    return null\n  } catch {\n    return null\n  }\n}\n\nfunction writeCachedConsumer(c: Consumer | null) {\n  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return\n  if (c) localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(c))\n  else localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY)\n  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_EVENT))\n}\n\nexport function ConsumerAuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [state, setState] = useState<AuthState>({ status: 'loading', consumer: null })\n  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)\n  const pendingAction = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null)\n\n  // Hydrate from cache instantly, then revalidate against the server cookie\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const cached = readCachedConsumer()\n    if (cached) setState({ status: 'authenticated', consumer: cached })\n\n    let cancelled = false\n    fetch('/api/consumer/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' })\n      .then((r) => r.json())\n      .then((json) => {\n        if (cancelled) return\n        const c: Consumer | null = json?.consumer ?? null\n        if (c) {\n          writeCachedConsumer(c)\n          setState({ status: 'authenticated', consumer: c })\n        } else {\n          writeCachedConsumer(null)\n          setState({ status: 'anonymous', consumer: null })\n        }\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        if (cancelled) return\n        if (!cached) setState({ status: 'anonymous', consumer: null })\n      })\n\n    const sync = () => {\n      const next = readCachedConsumer()\n      setState(next\n        ? { status: 'authenticated', consumer: next }\n        : { status: 'anonymous', consumer: null })\n    }\n    window.addEventListener(AUTH_EVENT, sync)\n    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true\n      window.removeEventListener(AUTH_EVENT, sync)\n      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)\n    }\n  }, [])\n\n  const handleAuthSuccess = useCallback((consumer: Consumer) => {\n    writeCachedConsumer(consumer)\n    setState({ status: 'authenticated', consumer })\n    setModalOpen(false)\n    const action = pendingAction.current\n    pendingAction.current = null\n    if (action) {\n      // Run on next tick so the modal can finish closing first\n      setTimeout(action, 0)\n    }\n  }, [])\n\n  const login = useCallback(async ({ phone, password }: LoginPayload): Promise<AuthResult> => {\n    const r = await fetch('/api/consumer/login', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      credentials: 'same-origin',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ phone, password }),\n    })\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok || !json?.ok || !json?.consumer) {\n      return { ok: false, error: json?.error ?? 'Login failed.' }\n    }\n    handleAuthSuccess(json.consumer)\n    return { ok: true }\n  }, [handleAuthSuccess])\n\n  const register = useCallback(async (payload: RegisterPayload): Promise<AuthResult> => {\n    const r = await fetch('/api/consumer/register', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      credentials: 'same-origin',\n      body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n    })\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok || !json?.ok || !json?.consumer) {\n      return { ok: false, error: json?.error ?? 'Sign-up failed.' }\n    }\n    handleAuthSuccess(json.consumer)\n    return { ok: true }\n  }, [handleAuthSuccess])\n\n  const logout = useCallback(async () => {\n    await fetch('/api/consumer/logout', { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)\n    writeCachedConsumer(null)\n    setState({ status: 'anonymous', consumer: null })\n  }, [])\n\n  const requireAuth = useCallback((action: () => void) => {\n    if (state.status === 'authenticated') {\n      action()\n      return\n    }\n    pendingAction.current = action\n    setModalOpen(true)\n  }, [state.status])\n\n  const openAuth = useCallback(() => setModalOpen(true), [])\n  const closeAuth = useCallback(() => {\n    pendingAction.current = null\n    setModalOpen(false)\n  }, [])\n\n  return (\n    <ConsumerAuthContext.Provider\n      value={{\n        state,\n        consumer: state.consumer,\n        requireAuth,\n        login,\n        register,\n        logout,\n        openAuth,\n        closeAuth,\n      }}\n    >\n      {children}\n      {modalOpen && <AuthModal />}\n    </ConsumerAuthContext.Provider>\n  )\n}\n\nexport function useConsumerAuth(): ConsumerAuthContextValue {\n  const ctx = useContext(ConsumerAuthContext)\n  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useConsumerAuth must be used inside <ConsumerAuthProvider>')\n  return ctx\n}\n"
}

 autoComplete=\"tel\"\n                value={phone}\n                onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value.replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(0, 10))}\n                placeholder=\"98765 43210\"\n <input\n                type={showPw ? > AGENT

**Step 5 done.** ✅

Summary:
- `src/lib/ConsumerAuthContext.tsx` — provider wrapping the app. Hydrates from localStorage instantly (no flash) then revalidates against `/api/consumer/me`. Exposes `requireAuth(action)`, `login`, `register`, `logout`, `consumer`.
- `src/components/consumer/AuthModal.tsx` — bottom-sheet modal, login + register tabs, bilingual labels, password show/hide, validation. Loaded lazily (`next/dynamic`) so it doesn't bloat the initial bundle.
- Wired into `src/app/layout.tsx`.
- Three "Add to cart" call sites now wrap the action in `requireAuth(() => …)`:
  - `consumer/page.tsx` (homepage produce list)
  - `BrowseProduceTab` (regional discovery)
  - `ProduceTab` (farmer profile)
- Cart's place-order buttons (UPI / COD / fallback COD) all gated through `requireAuth`.
- Cart prefills name + phone from the authenticated user.
- Browsing remains fully anonymous — modal only shows when user attempts a mutation.

---

**Step 6 next — Header greeting + logout in consumer nav.**

Plan:
- Edit `src/components/consumer/GlobalNav.tsx`:
  - Show "Hi {name} / హాయ్ {name}" inline when authenticated (next to logo or in a small chip on the right beside the language toggle)
  - Tapping the greeting opens a small dropdown with: "My orders", "Logout"
  - When anonymous, show a subtle "Login / లాగిన్" link that opens the auth modal via `openAuth()`
- Keep the role-tab row intact (Consumer / Farmer / Delivery / Moderator)

Proceeding now.

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/components/consumer/GlobalNav.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport Link from 'next/link'\nimport { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'\nimport { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'\nimport LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'\nimport { useConsumerAuth } from '@/lib/ConsumerAuthContext'\n\ntype ActiveTab = 'consumer' | 'farmer' | 'delivery' | 'moderator'\n\nexport default function GlobalNav({ activeTab = 'consumer' }: { activeTab?: ActiveTab }) {\n  const { tx } = useLang()\n  const { state, consumer, openAuth, logout } = useConsumerAuth()\n\n  const tabs = [\n    { key: 'consumer' as const, href: '/consumer', label: tx.consumerNav },\n    { key: 'farmer' as const, href: '/farmer/dashboard', label: tx.farmerNav },\n    { key: 'delivery' as const, href: '#', label: tx.deliveryNav, disabled: true },\n    { key: 'moderator' as const, href: '#', label: tx.moderatorNav, disabled: true },\n  ]\n\n  return (\n    <nav className=\"sticky top-0 z-50 bg-green-900 shadow-lg\">\n      {/* Logo row */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-green-800 gap-2\">\n        <Link href=\"/consumer\" className=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0\">\n          <div className=\"w-9 h-9 bg-green-700 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0\">\n            <span className=\"text-white font-black text-xs\">YFF</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"leading-tight min-w-0\">\n            <span className=\"text-white font-bold text-sm block truncate\">YourFamilyFarmer</span>\n            <span className=\"text-green-400 text-[11px] block truncate\">యువర్ ఫ్యామిలీ ఫార్మర్</span>\n          </div>\n        </Link>\n\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0\">\n          {state.status === 'loading' ? null : consumer ? (\n            <ConsumerMenu name={consumer.name} onLogout={logout} />\n          ) : (\n            <button\n              onClick={openAuth}\n              className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-green-100 bg-green-800 active:bg-green-700 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 leading-tight\"\n            >\n              Login / లాగిన్\n            </button>\n          )}\n          <LanguageToggle />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Role tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex\">\n        {tabs.map((tab) => {\n          const isActive = tab.key === activeTab\n\n          if (tab.disabled) {\n            return (\n              <span key={tab.key} className=\"flex-1 text-center py-2.5 text-xs text-green-700 font-medium\">\n                {tab.label}\n              </span>\n            )\n          }\n\n          return (\n            <Link\n              key={tab.key}\n              href={tab.href}\n              className={`flex-1 text-center py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${\n                isActive\n                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-b-2 border-green-300'\n                  : 'text-green-300 hover:text-white'\n              }`}\n            >\n              {tab.label}\n            </Link>\n          )\n        })}\n      </div>\n    </nav>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction ConsumerMenu({ name, onLogout }: { name: string | null; onLogout: () => Promise<void> }) {\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)\n  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)\n  const firstName = (name ?? '').split(' ')[0] || 'there'\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!open) return\n    const onDocClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (!wrapRef.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false)\n    }\n    const onEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false) }\n    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDocClick)\n    document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc)\n    return () => {\n      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDocClick)\n      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc)\n    }\n  }, [open])\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={wrapRef} className=\"relative\">\n      <button\n        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}\n        aria-haspopup=\"menu\"\n        aria-expanded={open}\n        className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-green-100 bg-green-800 active:bg-green-700 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 leading-tight max-w-[140px] truncate\"\n      >\n        Hi {firstName} ▾\n      </button>\n      {open && (\n        <div\n          role=\"menu\"\n          className=\"absolute right-0 mt-2 w-56 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100 py-1.5 text-sm z-50\"\n        >\n          <p className=\"px-4 py-2 text-[11px] text-gray-500 leading-tight border-b border-gray-100 mb-1\">\n            Logged in as<br />\n            <span className=\"text-gray-800 font-semibold\">{name || 'Consumer'}</span>\n          </p>\n          <Link\n            href=\"/consumer/orders\"\n            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}\n            className=\"block px-4 py-2.5 text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-100 font-semibold\"\n          >\n            🧾 My orders / నా ఆర్డర్లు\n          </Link>\n          <Link\n            href=\"/consumer\"\n            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}\n            className=\"block px-4 py-2.5 text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-100\"\n          >\n            🛒 Browse / కొనుగోలు\n          </Link>\n          <button\n            onClick={() => { setOpen(false); void onLogout() }}\n            className=\"block w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-red-600 active:bg-red-50 font-semibold border-t border-gray-100 mt-1\"\n          >\n            ↪ Log out / లాగౌట్\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

**Step 6 done.** ✅

`GlobalNav` now:
- Anonymous: shows a small "Login / లాగిన్" pill that opens the auth modal
- Authenticated: shows "Hi {firstName} ▾" pill; tap to open dropdown menu with My orders / Browse / Log out
- Closes on outside-click and Escape
- Ignores the brief loading state to avoid layout flicker

---

**Step 7 next — `orders.consumer_id` FK + place-order endpoint that uses session.**

Plan:
- **Migration**: add `orders.consumer_id uuid REFERENCES consumers_auth(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` + index
- **Server endpoint** `POST /api/orders/place` — accepts cart items (listingId, qty, farmerId), reads `consumer_id` from session cookie, validates: cart items exist, farmer owns each listing, prices computed server-side from DB (not trusted from client), payment_method respects farmer's `cod_enabled`, inserts orders rows with `consumer_id`, `buyer_name`, `buyer_phone` from the auth profile (not from form input).
- **Cart refactor**: replace the client-side `supabase.from('orders').insert(...)` calls (UPI + COD flows) with a `fetch('/api/orders/place', ...)` call. Pull `payment_method`, `pickup_location`, `pickup_day` from existing UI state. Endpoint returns inserted `orderIds` + computed totals.
- **Consumer orders page**: leave phone-input flow intact for now (Step 9 will refactor it to use session)

This is a significant refactor of the cart's order placement. Important to keep behavior: realtime farmer alerts still work (DB inserts happen, just on the server), UPI flow still moves to "I have paid" screen with `orderIds`, COD still triggers WhatsApp (oh wait — actually we already removed WhatsApp in the realtime commit; just save+toast).

Let me also be careful: the cart currently stores `pricePerKg` in `CartItem` from client memory. The endpoint must recompute total from current DB price + tier logic. I'll port the existing `getActiveTier` logic to the server.

Proceeding now.

  "content": "-- ================================================================\n-- YFF — Link orders to authenticated consumer accounts\n-- Run AFTER consumer-auth-migration.sql\n-- ================================================================\n\n-- 1. Foreign key — orders placed by registered consumers carry their account id.\n--    Older anonymous orders keep consumer_id = NULL. Account deletion sets to NULL\n--    so we never lose the order history a farmer needs.\nALTER TABLE orders\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS consumer_id uuid REFERENCES consumers_auth(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_consumer_id ON orders(consumer_id);\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/pricing.ts",
  "content": "// Tier-pricing helper, shared between the client cart and the server place-order\n// endpoint so the totals stay in lockstep no matter which side computes them.\n\nexport type TierInputs = {\n  pricePerKg?: number | null\n  priceTier1Qty?: number | null\n  priceTier1Price?: number | null\n  priceTier2Qty?: number | null\n  priceTier2Price?: number | null\n  priceTier3Price?: number | null\n}\n\nexport function getTierPrice(qty: number, item: TierInputs): number | null {\n  const {\n    pricePerKg,\n    priceTier1Qty,\n    priceTier1Price,\n    priceTier2Qty,\n    priceTier2Price,\n    priceTier3Price,\n  } = item\n\n  if (priceTier1Qty == null || priceTier1Price == null) {\n    return pricePerKg ?? null\n  }\n  if (qty <= priceTier1Qty) return priceTier1Price\n  if (priceTier2Qty != null && priceTier2Price != null) {\n    if (qty <= priceTier2Qty) return priceTier2Price\n    return priceTier3Price ?? priceTier2Price\n  }\n  if (priceTier3Price != null) return priceTier3Price\n  return priceTier1Price\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/orders/place/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'\nimport { getTierPrice } from '@/lib/pricing'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\ntype IncomingItem = { listingId: string; qty: number }\n\ntype ListingRow = {\n  id: string\n  name: string\n  unit: string | null\n  stock_qty: number | null\n  farmer_id: string\n  price_tier_1_qty: number | null\n  price_tier_1_price: number | null\n  price_tier_2_qty: number | null\n  price_tier_2_price: number | null\n  price_tier_3_price: number | null\n}\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\nfunction bad(msg: string, status = 400) {\n  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)\n  if (!session) return bad('Please log in to place an order.', 401)\n\n  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null) as\n    | {\n        farmerId?: string\n        paymentMethod?: string\n        pickupLocation?: string | null\n        pickupDay?: string | null\n        items?: IncomingItem[]\n      }\n    | null\n\n  if (!body) return bad('Invalid request body.')\n  const { farmerId, paymentMethod, pickupLocation, pickupDay, items } = body\n\n  if (!farmerId || !UUID_RE.test(farmerId)) return bad('Invalid farmer.')\n  if (paymentMethod !== 'upi' && paymentMethod !== 'cod') return bad('Invalid payment method.')\n  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return bad('Cart is empty.')\n  if (items.length > 50) return bad('Too many items.')\n  for (const it of items) {\n    if (!it || typeof it !== 'object') return bad('Invalid item.')\n    if (!it.listingId || !UUID_RE.test(it.listingId)) return bad('Invalid listing id.')\n    if (!Number.isFinite(it.qty) || it.qty <= 0 || it.qty > 10000) return bad('Invalid quantity.')\n  }\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Authoritative consumer profile (we never trust the client for buyer name/phone)\n  const { data: consumer } = await supabase\n    .from('consumers_auth')\n    .select('id, name, phone')\n    .eq('id', session.consumerId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!consumer) return bad('Account not found. Please log in again.', 401)\n\n  // Farmer COD acceptance check\n  const { data: farmer } = await supabase\n    .from('farmers')\n    .select('id, cod_enabled')\n    .eq('id', farmerId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!farmer) return bad('Farmer not found.', 404)\n  if (paymentMethod === 'cod' && farmer.cod_enabled !== true) {\n    return bad('This farmer is not accepting Cash on Delivery.')\n  }\n\n  // Pull live listing rows — never trust prices from the client cart\n  const listingIds = items.map((i) => i.listingId)\n  const { data: listings } = await supabase\n    .from('produce_listings')\n    .select(\n      'id, name, unit, stock_qty, farmer_id, price_tier_1_qty, price_tier_1_price, price_tier_2_qty, price_tier_2_price, price_tier_3_price',\n    )\n    .in('id', listingIds) as { data: ListingRow[] | null }\n\n  if (!listings || listings.length !== listingIds.length) {\n    return bad('One or more items in your cart are no longer available.')\n  }\n\n  const listingById = new Map(listings.map((l) => [l.id, l]))\n  const rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []\n  let total = 0\n\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)\n    if (!listing) return bad('Item missing.')\n    if (listing.farmer_id !== farmerId) return bad('Items must belong to the same farmer.')\n    if (listing.stock_qty != null && item.qty > listing.stock_qty) {\n      return bad(`Only ${listing.stock_qty} ${listing.unit || 'kg'} of ${listing.name} available right now.`)\n    }\n\n    const unitPrice = getTierPrice(item.qty, {\n      priceTier1Qty: listing.price_tier_1_qty,\n      priceTier1Price: listing.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier2Qty: listing.price_tier_2_qty,\n      priceTier2Price: listing.price_tier_2_price,\n      priceTier3Price: listing.price_tier_3_price,\n    })\n\n    const linePrice = unitPrice != null ? Math.round(unitPrice * item.qty) : null\n    if (linePrice == null || linePrice <= 0) {\n      return bad(`Price not set for ${listing.name}. Please ask the farmer.`)\n    }\n    total += linePrice\n\n    rows.push({\n      farmer_id: farmerId,\n      produce_listing_id: listing.id,\n      produce_name: listing.name,\n      quantity: item.qty,\n      unit: listing.unit || 'kg',\n      total_price: linePrice,\n      buyer_name: consumer.name || 'Buyer',\n      buyer_phone: consumer.phone,\n      consumer_id: consumer.id,\n      pickup_location: typeof pickupLocation === 'string' ? pickupLocation.slice(0, 200) : null,\n      pickup_day: typeof pickupDay === 'string' ? pickupDay.slice(0, 32) : null,\n      status: 'pending',\n      payment_method: paymentMethod,\n      payment_status: 'pending',\n    })\n  }\n\n  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .insert(rows)\n    .select('id')\n\n  if (insertErr || !inserted) {\n    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)\n    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({\n    ok: true,\n    orderIds: inserted.map((r) => r.id),\n    total,\n  })\n}\n"
}

Updated task #7 status
```

> AGENT

**Step 7 done.** ✅

Summary:
- **Migration** `scripts/orders-consumer-id-migration.sql` — `orders.consumer_id uuid REFERENCES consumers_auth(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` + index
- **Shared pricing module** `src/lib/pricing.ts` — single source of truth for tier price logic
- **Server endpoint** `POST /api/orders/place`:
  - Requires session cookie (returns 401 otherwise)
  - Validates farmerId/listingId UUID format, qty bounds (1–10000), max 50 items per order
  - Pulls the consumer's name/phone from `consumers_auth` (NOT from client body — client could lie)
  - Pulls live listings from DB and recomputes tier price + total — client-supplied prices ignored
  - Verifies all items belong to the requested farmer (no cross-farmer order injection)
  - Stock check: rejects if `qty > stock_qty`
  - Rejects COD when farmer's `cod_enabled = false`
  - Inserts orders with `consumer_id`, `buyer_name`, `buyer_phone`, `pickup_location`, `payment_method`, `payment_status='pending'`
- **Cart refactor**: both COD + UPI flows now call `/api/orders/place`. The cart no longer touches the `orders` table directly. Rotating to a phone-only ordering attack vector is closed: every order is bound to a logged-in `consumer_id`.
- Realtime farmer alerts still fire (server inserts trigger the same `postgres_changes` subscription)

---

**SQL to run before testing this step (cumulative):**
```sql
-- Step 3
ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cod_enabled boolean DEFAULT false;
UPDATE farmers SET cod_enabled = false WHERE cod_enabled IS NULL;

-- Step 4
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS consumers_auth (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name text,
  phone varchar(15) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  password_hash text NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now(),
  last_login_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_consumers_auth_phone ON consumers_auth(phone);
ALTER TABLE consumers_auth ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Step 7
ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS consumer_id uuid REFERENCES consumers_auth(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_consumer_id ON orders(consumer_id);
```
And one env var: `SESSION_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)`

Continuing to **Step 8 — Mandatory payment screenshot upload** next.

Plan for Step 8:

- **DB migration**: `ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_proof_path text;` (storage path, not a public URL)
- **Storage bucket**: private bucket `payment-proofs` (created via SQL or Supabase dashboard). Migration includes `INSERT INTO storage.buckets (id, name, public) VALUES ('payment-proofs','payment-proofs',false) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;`
- **Server endpoint** `POST /api/orders/upload-proof` — multipart/form-data:
  - Validates session
  - Verifies `orderId` belongs to this consumer
  - File: `image/jpeg`, `image/png`, `image/webp` only; ≤5MB
  - Uploads to `payment-proofs/{consumer_id}/{orderId}-{timestamp}.{ext}` via service role
  - Updates `orders.payment_proof_path`
  - Returns `{ok: true}`
- **Server endpoint** `GET /api/orders/[id]/proof` — returns a 5-min signed URL; only the order's buyer or farmer can fetch (we'll enforce buyer here; farmer endpoint when needed)
- **Cart UI**: add a mandatory file picker on the UPI payment screen between "Open UPI app" and "I Have Paid"; client-side compresses to 1200px @ 0.7 jpeg before upload; "I Have Paid" button is disabled until upload succeeds
- Add bilingual labels

Proceeding.

114	      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/imageCompress.ts",
  "content": "// Browser-side image compression. Used before uploading photos to Supabase\n// storage so we don't push 5MB phone-camera images over slow rural 4G.\nexport async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 1200, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {\n  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return file\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n    const img = new Image()\n    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)\n    img.onload = () => {\n      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)\n      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))\n      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')\n      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)\n      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')\n      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)\n      canvas.toBlob(\n        (blob) => {\n          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }\n          const name = file.name.replace(/\\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')\n          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))\n        },\n        'image/jpeg',\n        quality,\n      )\n    }\n    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }\n    img.src = blobUrl\n  })\n}\n"
}

The anon key has no policies on it, so only API routes\n-- (using the service role) can upload, list, or sign URLs.\nINSERT INTO storage.buckets (id, name, public)\nVALUES ('payment-proofs', 'payment-proofs', false)\nON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;\n"

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/orders/upload-proof/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\nconst ALLOWED_TYPES = new Set(['image/jpeg', 'image/jpg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'])\nconst MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024 // 5MB\n\nfunction bad(msg: string, status = 400) {\n  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)\n  if (!session) return bad('Please log in.', 401)\n\n  let form: FormData\n  try {\n    form = await req.formData()\n  } catch {\n    return bad('Invalid upload payload.')\n  }\n\n  const orderId = String(form.get('orderId') ?? '')\n  const file = form.get('file')\n\n  if (!orderId || !UUID_RE.test(orderId)) return bad('Invalid order id.')\n  if (!(file instanceof File)) return bad('No file attached.')\n  if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.has(file.type)) return bad('Only JPG, PNG, or WEBP screenshots are allowed.')\n  if (file.size === 0) return bad('Empty file.')\n  if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) return bad('Screenshot is too large. Max 5MB.')\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  // Confirm the order exists AND belongs to this consumer\n  const { data: order } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select('id, consumer_id')\n    .eq('id', orderId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!order) return bad('Order not found.', 404)\n  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) return bad('Not your order.', 403)\n\n  const ext = file.type === 'image/png' ? 'png'\n    : file.type === 'image/webp' ? 'webp'\n    : 'jpg'\n  const path = `${session.consumerId}/${orderId}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`\n\n  const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n    .from('payment-proofs')\n    .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: true })\n\n  if (upErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF] proof upload failed:', upErr.message)\n    return bad('Could not save screenshot. Please try again.', 500)\n  }\n\n  const { error: updErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .update({ payment_proof_path: path })\n    .eq('id', orderId)\n\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF] proof path save failed:', updErr.message)\n    return bad('Saved screenshot but could not link it. Please contact support.', 500)\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, path })\n}\n"
}
